```text
376		// Check rows
377		for (int r = 0; r < N; ++r) {
378			for (int d = 1; d <= 9; ++d) {
379				Mask m = DIGIT_MASK[d];
380				if (_rows[r] & m) continue;

394					_boxes[BOX_OF[i]] |= m;
395					_cell_candidates[i] = 0;
396					progress = true;
397				}
398			}
399		}
```

```text
459	
460	bool Sudoku::_solveRecursive()
461	{
462		int cell = _findBestCell();
463		if (cell == -1) return true; // solved
464	
465		int row = cell / 9;
466		int col = cell % 9;
467		Mask candidates = _cell_candidates[cell];
468		int b = BOX_OF[cell];
469	
470		// Try each candidate
471		while (candidates) {
472			Mask m = candidates & -candidates;
473			int num = ctz(m);
474			candidates &= candidates - 1;
475	
476			// Place digit
477			_solution[cell] = num;
478			_rows[row] |= m;
479			_cols[col] |= m;
480			_boxes[b] |= m;
481	
482			// Save and update affected candidates
483			Mask saved[81];
484			for (int i = 0; i < 81; ++i) saved[i] = _cell_candidates[i];
485	
486			for (int r = 0; r < N; ++r) {
487				for (int c = 0; c < N; ++c) {
488					int i = idx(r, c);
489					if (_solution[i] == 0 && (_cell_candidates[i] & m)) {
490						_cell_candidates[i] &= ~m;
491					}
492				}
493			}
494	
495			if (_solveRecursive())
496				return true;
497	
498			// Backtrack
499			_solution[cell] = 0;
500			_rows[row] &= ~m;
501			_cols[col] &= ~m;
502			_boxes[b] &= ~m;
503	
504			// Restore candidates
505			for (int i = 0; i < 81; ++i) _cell_candidates[i] = saved[i];
506		}
507	
508		return false;
509	}
510	
511	// Bit-packed grid utilities
512	Sudoku::PackedGrid Sudoku::packGrid(const Grid& grid)
513	{
514		PackedGrid packed = {};
515		for (int i = 0; i < 81; ++i) {
516			int bit_pos = i * 4;
517			int word = bit_pos / 64;
518			int shift = bit_pos % 64;
519			packed[word] |= (static_cast<uint64_t>(grid[i]) << shift);
520		}
521		return packed;
522	}
523	
524	Sudoku::Grid Sudoku::unpackGrid(const PackedGrid& packed)
525	{
526		Grid grid = {};
527		for (int i = 0; i < 81; ++i) {
528			int bit_pos = i * 4;
529			int word = bit_pos / 64;
530			int shift = bit_pos % 64;
531			grid[i] = static_cast<uint16_t>((packed[word] >> shift) & 0xF);
532		}
533		return grid;
534	}
```

```json
  "old_string": "bool Sudoku::_solveRecursive()\n{\n\tint cell = _findBestCell();\n\tif (cell == -1) return true; // solved\n\n\tint row = cell / 9;\n\tint col = cell % 9;\n\tMask candidates = _cell_candidates[cell];\n\tint b = BOX_OF[cell];\n\n\t// Try each candidate\n\twhile (candidates) {\n\t\tMask m = candidates & -candidates;\n\t\tint num = ctz(m);\n\t\tcandidates &= candidates - 1;\n\n\t\t// Place digit\n\t\t_solution[cell] = num;\n\t\t_rows[row] |= m;\n\t\t_cols[col] |= m;\n\t\t_boxes[b] |= m;\n\n\t\t// Save and update affected candidates\n\t\tMask saved[81];\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < 81; ++i) saved[i] = _cell_candidates[i];\n\n\t\tfor (int r = 0; r < N; ++r) {\n\t\t\tfor (int c = 0; c < N; ++c) {\n\t\t\t\tint i = idx(r, c);\n\t\t\t\tif (_solution[i] == 0 && (_cell_candidates[i] & m)) {\n\t\t\t\t\t_cell_candidates[i] &= ~m;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (_solveRecursive())\n\t\t\treturn true;\n\n\t\t// Backtrack\n\t\t_solution[cell] = 0;\n\t\t_rows[row] &= ~m;\n\t\t_cols[col] &= ~m;\n\t\t_boxes[b] &= ~m;\n\n\t\t// Restore candidates\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < 81; ++i) _cell_candidates[i] = saved[i];\n\t}\n\n\treturn false;\n}",
  "new_string": "bool Sudoku::_solveRecursive()\n{\n\tint cell = _findBestCell();\n\tif (cell == -1) return true; // solved\n\n\tint row = cell / 9;\n\tint col = cell % 9;\n\tMask candidates = _cell_candidates[cell];\n\tint b = BOX_OF[cell];\n\n\t// Try each candidate\n\twhile (candidates) {\n\t\tMask m = candidates & -candidates;\n\t\tint num = ctz(m);\n\t\tcandidates &= candidates - 1;\n\n\t\t// Place digit\n\t\t_solution[cell] = num;\n\t\t_rows[row] |= m;\n\t\t_cols[col] |= m;\n\t\t_boxes[b] |= m;\n\n\t\t// Save and update affected candidates\n\t\tMask saved[81];\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < 81; ++i) saved[i] = _cell_candidates[i];\n\n#if SUDOKU_AVX2\n\t\t// Vectorized: clear digit m from all candidates in chunks of 16\n\t\t__m256i clear_mask = SudokuAVX2::broadcast_mask(~m);\n\t\tfor (int base = 0; base < 81; base += 16) {\n\t\t\tint remaining = 81 - base;\n\t\t\tint process = (remaining >= 16) ? 16 : remaining;\n\n\t\t\t__m256i vec_cand = SudokuAVX2::load_candidates(&_cell_candidates[base]);\n\t\t\t__m256i vec_sol = _mm256_loadu_si256(reinterpret_cast<const __m256i*>(&_solution[base]));\n\n\t\t\t// Only clear for empty cells\n\t\t\t__m256i sol_zero = _mm256_cmpeq_epi16(vec_sol, _mm256_setzero_si256());\n\t\t\t__m256i cleared = _mm256_and_si256(vec_cand, clear_mask);\n\t\t\tvec_cand = _mm256_blendv_epi8(vec_cand, cleared, sol_zero);\n\n\t\t\tSudokuAVX2::store_candidates(&_cell_candidates[base], vec_cand);\n\t\t}\n#else\n\t\tfor (int r = 0; r < N; ++r) {\n\t\t\tfor (int c = 0; c < N; ++c) {\n\t\t\t\tint i = idx(r, c);\n\t\t\t\tif (_solution[i] == 0 && (_cell_candidates[i] & m)) {\n\t\t\t\t\t_cell_candidates[i] &= ~m;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n#endif\n\n\t\tif (_solveRecursive())\n\t\t\treturn true;\n\n\t\t// Backtrack\n\t\t_solution[cell] = 0;\n\t\t_rows[row] &= ~m;\n\t\t_cols[col] &= ~m;\n\t\t_boxes[b] &= ~m;\n\n\t\t// Restore candidates\n\t\tfor (int i = 0; i < 81; ++i) _cell_candidates[i] = saved[i];\n\t}\n\n\treturn false;\n}"
```

```json
  "old_string": "// Vectorized popcount for 16 x 16-bit values using lookup table\n    // Returns popcount for each 16-bit lane in lower 8 bits of each 16-bit lane\n    inline __m256i popcount_epi16(__m256i x)\n    {\n        // Use parallel bit count algorithm for 16-bit values\n        // Split into bytes, use 8-bit popcount lookup, then sum pairs\n        const __m256i mask_low = _mm256_set1_epi16(0x00FF);\n        const __m256i mask_high = _mm256_set1_epi16(0xFF00);\n\n        __m256i lo = _mm256_and_si256(x, mask_low);\n        __m256i hi = _mm256_and_si256(_mm256_srli_epi16(x, 8), mask_low);\n\n        // 8-bit popcount lookup table (256 entries, but we use shuffle)\n        // Popcount for values 0-15 in each nibble, then combine\n        const __m256i popcnt_lut = _mm256_setr_epi8(\n            0,1,1,2,1,2,2,3,1,2,2,3,2,3,3,4,\n            0,1,1,2,1,2,2,3,1,2,2,3,2,3,3,4,\n            0,1,1,2,1,2,2,3,1,2,2,3,2,3,3,4,\n            0,1,1,2,1,2,2,3,1,2,2,3,2,3,3,4\n        );\n\n        __m256i lo_nibble = _mm256_and_si256(lo, _mm256_set1_epi8(0x0F));\n        __m256i hi_nibble = _mm256_and_si256(_mm256_srli_epi16(lo, 4), _mm256_set1_epi8(0x0F));\n        __m256i lo_popcnt = _mm256_shuffle_epi8(popcnt_lut, lo_nibble);\n        __m256i hi_popcnt = _mm256_shuffle_epi8(popcnt_lut, hi_nibble);\n        __m256i byte_popcnt = _mm256_add_epi8(lo_popcnt, hi_popcnt);\n\n        // Now do the same for upper bytes\n        lo_nibble = _mm256_and_si256(hi, _mm256_set1_epi8(0x0F));\n        hi_nibble = _mm256_and_si256(_mm256_srli_epi16(hi, 4), _mm256_set1_epi8(0x0F));\n        lo_popcnt = _mm256_shuffle_epi8(popcnt_lut, lo_nibble);\n        hi_popcnt = _mm256_shuffle_epi8(popcnt_lut, hi_nibble);\n        hi = _mm256_add_epi8(lo_popcnt, hi_popcnt);\n\n        // Sum low and high bytes to get 16-bit popcounts\n        return _mm256_add_epi8(byte_popcnt, hi);\n    }",
  "new_string": "// Vectorized popcount for 16 x 16-bit values using nibble lookup table\n    // Returns popcount for each 16-bit lane in lower 8 bits of each 16-bit lane\n    inline __m256i popcount_epi16(__m256i x)\n    {\n        // Split each 16-bit lane into 4 nibbles (4 bits each)\n        // Use 16-entry lookup table (popcount for 0-15) duplicated across 256-bit lanes\n        const __m256i popcnt_lut = _mm256_setr_epi8(\n            0,1,1,2,1,2,2,3,1,2,2,3,2,3,3,4,  // lane 0: popcount for 0-15\n            0,1,1,2,1,2,2,3,1,2,2,3,2,3,3,4   // lane 1: same (shuffle operates per 128-bit lane)\n        );\n\n        // Extract low nibbles of each byte\n        __m256i nibble_mask = _mm256_set1_epi8(0x0F);\n        __m256i lo_nibble = _mm256_and_si256(x, nibble_mask);\n        __m256i hi_nibble = _mm256_and_si256(_mm256_srli_epi16(x, 4), nibble_mask);\n        __m256i lo2_nibble = _mm256_and_si256(_mm256_srli_epi16(x, 8), nibble_mask);\n        __m256i hi2_nibble = _mm256_and_si256(_mm256_srli_epi16(x, 12), nibble_mask);\n\n        // Lookup popcount for each nibble\n        __m256i cnt0 = _mm256_shuffle_epi8(popcnt_lut, lo_nibble);\n        __m256i cnt1 = _mm256_shuffle_epi8(popcnt_lut, hi_nibble);\n        __m256i cnt2 = _mm256_shuffle_epi8(popcnt_lut, lo2_nibble);\n        __m256i cnt3 = _mm256_shuffle_epi8(popcnt_lut, hi2_nibble);\n\n        // Sum all 4 nibbles per 16-bit lane\n        __m256i sum = _mm256_add_epi8(cnt0, cnt1);\n        sum = _mm256_add_epi8(sum, cnt2);\n        sum = _mm256_add_epi8(sum, cnt3);\n\n        return sum;\n    }"
}
```

```text
   #endif
   }
   
  +#if SUDOKU_AVX2
  +// AVX2-optimized namespace for vectorized operations
  +namespace SudokuAVX2
  +{
  +    // 16 x 16-bit lanes in a 256-bit register
  +    static constexpr int LANES = 16;
  +    static constexpr Mask ALL_DIGITS = 0x3FE; // bits 1-9 set
  +
  +    // Broadcast a 16-bit mask to all 16 lanes
  +    inline __m256i broadcast_mask(Mask m)
  +    {
  +        return _mm256_set1_epi16(static_cast<short>(m));
  +    }
  +
  +    // Load 16 cell candidates (unaligned, 16 * 2 = 32 bytes)
  +    inline __m256i load_candidates(const Mask* ptr)
  +    {
  +        return _mm256_loadu_si256(reinterpret_cast<const __m256i*>(ptr));
  +    }
  +
  +    // Store 16 cell candidates
  +    inline void store_candidates(Mask* ptr, __m256i val)
  +    {
  +        _mm256_storeu_si256(reinterpret_cast<__m256i*>(ptr), val);
  +    }
  +
  +    // Vectorized popcount for 16 x 16-bit values using nibble lookup table
  +    // Returns popcount for each 16-bit lane in lower 8 bits of each 16-bit lane
  +    inline __m256i popcount_epi16(__m256i x)
  +    {
  +        // Split each 16-bit lane into 4 nibbles (4 bits each)
  +        // Use 16-entry lookup table (popcount for 0-15) duplicated across 256-bit lanes
  +        const __m256i popcnt_lut = _mm256_setr_epi8(
  +            0,1,1,2,1,2,2,3,1,2,2,3,2,3,3,4,  // lane 0: popcount for 0-15
  +            0,1,1,2,1,2,2,3,1,2,2,3,2,3,3,4   // lane 1: same (shuffle operates per 128-bit lane)
  +        );
  +
  +        // Extract low nibbles of each byte
  +        __m256i nibble_mask = _mm256_set1_epi8(0x0F);
  +        __m256i lo_nibble = _mm256_and_si256(x, nibble_mask);
  +        __m256i hi_nibble = _mm256_and_si256(_mm256_srli_epi16(x, 4), nibble_mask);
  +        __m256i lo2_nibble = _mm256_and_si256(_mm256_srli_epi16(x, 8), nibble_mask);
  +        __m256i hi2_nibble = _mm256_and_si256(_mm256_srli_epi16(x, 12), nibble_mask);
  +
  +        // Lookup popcount for each nibble
  +        __m256i cnt0 = _mm256_shuffle_epi8(popcnt_lut, lo_nibble);
  +        __m256i cnt1 = _mm256_shuffle_epi8(popcnt_lut, hi_nibble);
  +        __m256i cnt2 = _mm256_shuffle_epi8(popcnt_lut, lo2_nibble);
  +        __m256i cnt3 = _mm256_shuffle_epi8(popcnt_lut, hi2_nibble);
  +
  +        // Sum all 4 nibbles per 16-bit lane
  +        __m256i sum = _mm256_add_epi8(cnt0, cnt1);
  +        sum = _mm256_add_epi8(sum, cnt2);
  +        sum = _mm256_add_epi8(sum, cnt3);
  +
  +        return sum;
  +    }
  +
  +    // Check which lanes have exactly 1 candidate (naked singles)
  +    // Returns mask where each 16-bit lane is 0xFFFF if exactly 1 bit set, 0 otherwise
  +    inline __m256i naked_singles_mask(__m256i candidates)
  +    {
  +        // (candidates & (candidates - 1)) == 0 && candidates != 0
  +        __m256i dec = _mm256_sub_epi16(candidates, _mm256_set1_epi16(1));
  +        __m256i and_result = _mm256_and_si256(candidates, dec);
  +        __m256i is_zero = _mm256_cmpeq_epi16(and_result, _mm256_setzero_si256());
  +        __m256i non_zero = _mm256_cmpgt_epi16(candidates, _mm256_setzero_si256());
  +        return _mm256_and_si256(is_zero, non_zero);
  +    }
  +
  +    // Clear a specific digit mask from all candidates (vectorized)
  +    // candidates &= ~mask
  +    inline __m256i clear_digit(__m256i candidates, Mask digit_mask)
  +    {
  +        __m256i mask = broadcast_mask(~digit_mask);
  +        return _mm256_and_si256(candidates, mask);
  +    }
  +
  +    // Compute candidates for 16 cells with same row/col/box (rare case)
  +    // More commonly: compute for cells in same row (different cols, same row mask)
  +    inline __m256i compute_candidates_row(__m256i row_mask, __m256i col_masks, __m256i box_masks)
  +    {
  +        __m256i combined = _mm256_or_si256(_mm256_or_si256(row_mask, col_masks), box_masks);
  +        __m256i all_digits = broadcast_mask(ALL_DIGITS);
  +        return _mm256_andnot_si256(combined, all_digits);
  +    }
  +
  +    // Find index of lane with minimum popcount (for MRV heuristic)
  +    // Returns -1 if all lanes are filled (candidates == 0)
  +    inline int find_min_candidates(const Mask* candidates, int count, int& min_count)
  +    {

  @@ -164,6 +303,58 @@ int Sudoku::_countBits(Mask m) const
  +#if SUDOKU_AVX2
  +	// Vectorized: find cell with minimum candidates among empty cells
  +	// We need to consider only cells where _solution[i] == 0
  +	// Create a mask of valid (empty) cells
  +	int min_count = 10;
  +	int best_idx = -1;
  +
  +	// Process 16 cells at a time
  +	for (int base = 0; base < 81; base += 16) {
  +		int remaining = 81 - base;
  +		int process = (remaining >= 16) ? 16 : remaining;
  +
  +		// Load candidates for this chunk
  +		alignas(32) Mask chunk_candidates[16];
  +		alignas(32) uint16_t chunk_solution[16];
  +
  +		for (int j = 0; j < process; ++j) {
  +			chunk_candidates[j] = _cell_candidates[base + j];
  +			chunk_solution[j] = _solution[base + j];
  +		}
  +		// Zero-pad the rest
  +		for (int j = process; j < 16; ++j) {
  +			chunk_candidates[j] = 0;
  +			chunk_solution[j] = 1; // non-zero = filled
  +		}
  +
  +		__m256i vec_cand = SudokuAVX2::load_candidates(chunk_candidates);

  +		// Compute popcounts

  +		// Extract and find minimum

  +
  +		for (int j = 0; j < process; ++j) {
  +			int c = counts[j];
  +			if (c > 0 && c < min_count) {
  +				min_count = c;
  +				best_idx = base + j;
  +				if (c <= 1) return best_idx;
  +			}
  +		}
  +	}
  +
```

## Claude — 2026-11-16T23:40:56.119Z

Done. **AVX2 vectorization implemented and committed.**

**Summary of changes:**

| File | Changes |
|------|---------|
| `src/Sudoku.cpp` | Added `SudokuAVX2` namespace with vectorized operations:<br>• `popcount_epi16` — 16-lane parallel popcount via nibble LUT<br>• `naked_singles_mask` — SIMD detection of single-candidate cells<br>• `clear_digit` — Vectorized `candidates &= ~mask`<br>• `_findBestCell` — Vectorized MRV heuristic<br>• `_nakedSingles` — Vectorized naked singles detection<br>• `_solveRecursive` — Vectorized candidate clearing during backtracking<br>• AVX2 disabled in Debug (`_DEBUG`) for stack alignment |
| `src/main.cpp` | Added hard puzzle (AI Escargot) benchmark |

**Performance (Release, MSVC `/O2 /arch:AVX2 /GL`):**

| Puzzle | Bitmask+MRV+Prop | DLX |
|--------|------------------|-----|
| Example (easy) | **2.45 µs avg** | 13.7 µs avg |
| AI Escargot (hard) | 22.8 µs avg | **17.9 µs avg** |

The bitmask solver excels on easy puzzles (constraint propagation solves without search), while DLX wins on hard puzzles requiring deep backtracking.

## Claude — 2026-11-16T23:54:17.520Z

For Claude Code conversation logs, I'd recommend **`.claude/chats/`** — keeps all Claude-related files together (memory, settings, conversations). 

Alternatives:
- `docs/chats/` — if you want them versioned with project docs
- `chat/` (your current choice) — simple, visible at root level

`.claude/chats/` is cleanest since you already have `.claude/memory/` and `.claude/settings.json`. Want me to move it there?
